# 📚 论文日报 · 翻译智能体 & Agent

本仓库每天 11:00 由本地程序自动更新：抓取 arXiv 关键词论文 + Hugging Face 本周热门论文。

表格里每篇论文有一条大模型根据**摘要**写的筛选笔记，分三段：**做法**、**作者报告**（摘要里的结果和数字，是作者的声称，不是已经证实的结论）、**判断**（一眼能看出的局限，或和我的关系）。筛选笔记只读摘要不读全文，所以它不能代替对证据的核查。

**相关度**：⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ 论文本身就在做我的研究方向；⭐⭐⭐⭐ 不做这些方向，但方法或工具能直接用上；⭐⭐⭐ 有间接借鉴；⭐⭐ 只是同属一个大领域；⭐ 基本无关。

每天另有 8 篇论文的**全文精读**（逐条核查主张与证据），在当天 Issue 的评论里。README 的表格点开可展开英文原摘要。

点右上角 **Watch** → **Custom** → 勾选 **Issues** 可以每天收到邮件通知。

关键词、数量等配置在 [`config.py`](config.py) 里修改。

Last update: 2026-11-24

| **[TeleChat to Xing4: Advancing Frontier Agentic Models on Domestic Chips and Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22115)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22115) | 2026-11-17 | **167** 👍 | **做法**：华为昇腾上的285B模型Xing 4系列技术报告，涵盖架构、预训练设施和TeleGym智能体执行平台。<br>**作者报告**：无效果声称<br>**判断**：2星偏3星：TeleGym智能体训练平台对方向4有参考价值，但主体是基础设施。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>We present Xing 4, a series of large language models that extends the TeleChat lineage through coordinated advances in model architecture and training infrastructure on Ascend. We study how sparse activation, attention design, model depth and width, and speculative decoding balance model capacity with computation, memory access, and execution overhead. Our pretraining infrastructure builds on MindSpore HyperParallel, combining topology-aware hybrid sharding, efficient distributed optimizer execution, and coordinated management of long-sequence workloads and activation memory. CPU affinity and core isolation</p></details> |
| **[Jeveration: System One Models Now Generate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24738)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.24738) | 2026-11-18 | **152** 👍 | **做法**：Jeveration用冻结的视觉语言模型单次前向生成图像，通过共享随机状态协调并行预测保持全局一致。<br>**作者报告**：512x512图像78ms生成，比现有统一模型快122倍，图像质量和密集预测有竞争力。<br>**判断**：可跳过：图像生成方向，与四个方向均无关。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>System One models such as Jev return decisions through fast, parallel prediction. Jeveration builds on a frozen vision-language model, repurposing its visual-semantic knowledge for holistic image generation. The key challenge is to maintain global consistency. A single prompt admits many valid images, yet parallel predictions must converge on a coherent whole. We therefore condition all positions on a shared random state to coordinate parallel predictions, so that each draw yields a different but coherent image. | **[cmHC: Balancing Stream Mixing in Hyper-Connections via Geometric Contraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27193)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27193) | 2026-11-20 | **94** 👍 | **做法**：改进 hyper-connections 残差连接的流混合，提出 cmHC 对 Birkhoff polytope 做几何收缩，稳定超大规模预训练梯度传播。<br>**作者报告**：在多规模 dense 和 MoE 模型上有效且可扩展；Ascend NPU 融合算子降低 hyper-connection 延迟最高 44.8%。<br>**判断**：可跳过：纯架构预训练工作，与四个方向均无关。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Manifold-constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) extends residual connections to multiple streams and stabilizes residual propagation by constraining stream-mixing residual matrices to the Birkhoff polytope, the set of doubly stochastic matrices. In this work, we investigate how to effectively mix residual streams within the Birkhoff polytope. We reveal a mixing trade-off: when under-mixed residual matrices stay close to the vertices of the Birkhoff polytope, hyper-connections will degenerate into a set of nearly independent streams; when over-mixed residual matrices are concentrated around the uniform barycenter of the polytope, streams will become less distinguishable from each other. We theoretically validate our findings by bounding the cumulative residual mixing, stream difference and gra</p></details> |
| **[AscendGYM: A Cost-Aware Kernel Optimization Benchmark for Real-World Workloads on Ascend NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17621)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.17621) | 2026-11-13 | **94** 👍 | **做法**：AscendGYM是昇腾NPU内核优化基准，48个内核配真实负载的测试用例，提出STS指标衡量提速与token成本。<br>**作者报告**：无效果声称<br>**判断**：可跳过：系统内核优化基准，与研究方向基本无关。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Kernel optimization is essential for efficient large language model (LLM) training and inference on Ascend NPUs. To address this requirement, we introduce AscendGYM, a benchmark whose initial suite comprises 48 kernels across three difficulty levels. Each kernel is paired with test cases whose data types and tensor shapes are collected from real-world training and inference tasks. Consequently, performance improvements on AscendGYM map directly to the corresponding workloads. In terms of usability, two evaluation modes are supported in AscendGYM: model evaluation and model-harness evalu</p></details> |
| **[Mind2Act World: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Embodied Mind, Physical Action, and Mind-Action Composition in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26444)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26444) | 2026-11-19 | **56** 👍 | **做法**：Mind2Act World基准将机器人操作拆成Mind（决策）、Act（执行）、Mind2Act（闭环组合）三个视角做诊断。<br>**作者报告**：最强agent在Mind成功率52.7%、Act 39.0%，Mind2Act仅12.0%。<br>**判断**：可跳过：具身机器人评测，与研究方向无关。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Embodied intelligence aims to let robots perform complex physical tasks with human-like adaptability and reliability, which requires three complementary capabilities: history-based cognitive reasoning, precise and timely physical execution, and the composition of reasoning and execution through environmental feedback. Yet existing benchmarks either evaluate reasoning and execution within the same tasks or assess them separately without their composition, so they reveal little about whether a failure comes from reasoning, execution, or their composition. We introduce Mind2Act World, a diagnostic benchmark of 30 simulated manipulation tasks from five real-world application domains. Each task has three difficulty levels and two scene settings (Clean and Random), yielding 180 configurations. T</p></details> |
| **[Weight-Space Programming Doesn't Need Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22055)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22055) | 2026-11-17 | **56** 👍 | **做法**：让LLM不用梯度手写权重，三个模型自发选择把文本事实编成稀疏表加固定查询的架构。<br>**作者报告**：LLM设计的模型比同LLM写的Transformer准确率更高，参数少约1800倍，GPU上快17倍。<br>**判断**：可跳过：新颖但属模型架构探索，与研究方向无关。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Standard neural architectures such as the Transformer were chosen because they train well with gradient descent and run fast on GPUs. Given minimal guidance, ten labeled examples, and a sandbox, mainly on multiple-choice questions, we task three frontier LLMs with either generating the weights of a fixed architecture or designing the architecture themselves. When free to design, all three compile plain-text facts into a sparse table queried by a fixed procedure, reaching up to 54.4% test accuracy. An LLM-designed model achieves higher test accuracy than the most accurate released Transformer that the same </p></details> |
| **[Non-penetrating World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27963)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27963) | 2026-11-20 | **55** 👍 | **做法**：研究交互式视频世界模型如何避免物体穿透，发现 DiT 隐藏状态可预测动作安全性，训练轻量预测头拦截不安全动作。<br>**作者报告**：六个模型平均物理违规率从 74% 降至 16%，对开放域图像、第三人称视角和移动障碍零样本泛化。<br>**判断**：可跳过：视频世界模型物理安全，与研究方向无关。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>We study a new problem of preventing object penetration in interactive video world models. To understand how existing models handle this, we introduce NoPeBench, a benchmark that evaluates how world models respond to physical obstacles. Our evaluation reveals that none of the open-source models can reliably prevent such failures. Surprisingly, however, these models already encode the information needed to predict such violations. We find that the hidden states of diffusion transformers (DiTs) can predict whether a candidate action will penetrate an obstacle. Building on this, we introduce NoPeWorld, a lightweight framework that detects and prevents penetration. Specifically, we train a small prediction head on top of a frozen DiT's hidden states to read out if a candidate action is safe or</p></details> |
| **[WeVisForensics: Self-Evolving Agent for Omni AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21373)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21373) | 2026-11-16 | **55** 👍 | **做法**：WeVisForensics用主调查agent协调可自演化的取证子agent库检测AI生成视频，DTPO双时间尺度策略优化。<br>**作者报告**：域内外及agent基准平均检测准确率95.2%，比最强基线高9.0%。<br>**判断**：多agent自演化+双时间尺度RL的设计对方向4有借鉴。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Recent advances in video generation call for reliable detection grounded in explicit forensic evidence. However, current methods primarily depend on single-pass chain-of-thought reasoning that overlooks brief or localized artifacts, or on predefined multi-agent workflows that offer limited flexibility in selecting relevant inspections. Even with adaptive investigation, optimizing the investigation policy alone does not directly improve the forensic capabilities of fixed experts. We introduce WeVisForensics, an agentic framework in which a main investigator agent coordinates a self-evolving library of forensic inspector sub-agents spanning visual and audio cues. The investigator learns which artifact type to examine, over which window, and when to conclude, and these interactions provide sh</p></details> |
| **[WeMM-Video: Pushing the Frontiers of Open-Source Omni-Modal Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26409)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26409) | 2026-11-19 | **50** 👍 | **做法**：发布中英双语全模态视频理解基础模型家族 WeMM-Video（2B 到 35B-A3B），统一文本图像音视频处理，支持思考与非思考两种模式。<br>**作者报告**：120 多个基准上开源全模态视频理解领先，35B-A3B 超 Qwen3.5-Omni-Flash 等，9B 超 Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B。<br>**判断**：3 星：全模态底座模型，若未来做图文/PPT 翻译可当视觉底座。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Video understanding, particularly with accompanying audio, remains a central challenge in multimodal intelligence. In this report, we introduce WeMM-Video, a family of Chinese-English bilingual omni-modal foundation models spanning 2B, 4B, 9B, and 35B-A3B variants. Centering on joint audio-visual perception and reasoning, WeMM-Video unifies text, image, audio, and video processing within a hybrid-thinking paradigm, seamlessly supporting both a non-thinking instruct mode for fast, direct responses and a thinking mode for explicit step-by-step reasoning. The models are trained on a large-scale multimodal corpus of approximately 1.2 trillion tokens through progressive pre-training and a multi-stage post-training pipeline incorporating multi-teacher OPD, anti-repetition MrDPO, and specialist R</p></details> |
| **[Multi-Branch Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19800)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.19800) | 2026-11-15 | **50** 👍 | **做法**：提出 Multi-Branch Transformer，并行分支处理输入后融合，并设计 Joint-KV 共享缓存降低长上下文推理开销。<br>**作者报告**：同参数同算力下优于顺序 Transformer，标准架构需多至 1.26 倍算力才匹配；KV 缓存省 34%，解码吞吐高 51.4%。<br>**判断**：可跳过：模型架构工作，与研究方向无直接关系。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Transformer language models are commonly scaled by making their blocks wider or stacking them deeper. In this work, we explore a complementary path: branching, the parallel composition of full Transformer blocks. We introduce the Multi-Branch Transformer (MBT), which processes the input through shared initial layers, executes independently parameterized branches of sequential blocks in parallel, and fuses their outputs before shared final layers. At comparable training compute, we study three MBT variants: Wide-Shallow preserves block width, Narrow-Deep preserves sequential depth, and Balanced interpolates between them. In controlled pretraining experiments at four mixture-of-experts scales (0.8B to 2.1B total parameters), 2-branch Wide-Shallow MBTs consistently outperform sequential Trans</p></details> |
| **[ForceHarness: Turning Force into Action and Physics for Frozen Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21099)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21099) | 2026-11-16 | **45** 👍 | **做法**：让 API-only 冻结多模态模型利用力觉信息：自终止力原语闭环控制加物理量翻译层，把力轨迹转为模型可读的工具结果。<br>**作者报告**：真实机械臂上 GPT-6 Astra 零样本成功率从 3.3% 升到 90.0%；ForceBench 加 17.5 点，HiddenPhys 加 34.3 点。<br>**判断**：4 星：'把原始信号翻译成 LLM 可推理形式'的思路对工具调用设计有借鉴。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Frontier multimodal models can now control robots zero-shot from images, yet many manipulation tasks depend on contact forces that images cannot show. Bringing force to an API-only frozen model raises two challenges. First, contact evolves much faster than an API model can decide, so the system must close the loop on force beneath the model. Second, a frozen model cannot interpret raw force streams, so force must be returned in a form the model can reason about. Therefore, we present ForceHarness, a harness that lets frozen models use force without demonstrations or finetuning. To address these challenges for Indic languages, we introduce Pralekha, a benchmark containing over 3 million aligned document pairs across 11 Indic languages and English, which includes 1.5 million English-Indic pair</p></details> |
| **[DocHPLT: A Massively Multilingual Document-Level Translation Dataset](http://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13079v2)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2508.13079v2) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>WMT 2025</p></details> | 2025-09-29 | **做法**：构建50语言、1.24亿对齐文档对的DocHPLT数据集，保留文档完整性而非句级拼接。<br>**作者报告**：42.6亿句；微调后LLM大幅超过现成指令模型，低资源语言改进尤其明显；摘要未给具体数字。<br>**判断**：最大文档级翻译数据集，方向直接相关的基础资源。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Existing document-level machine translation resources are only available for a handful of languages, mostly high-resourced ones. | **[Document-Level Text Generation with Minimum Bayes Risk Decoding using Optimal Transport](http://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23078v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2505.23078v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>ACL 2025</p></details> | 2025-05-29 | **做法**：MBR-OT用Wasserstein距离把句级效用函数组合成文档级效用，做文档级生成的MBR解码。<br>**作者报告**：在文档级翻译、文本简化、密集图像描述任务上超标准MBR；摘要未给具体数字。<br>**判断**：文档翻译解码策略，方法可迁移到我的DocMT评测。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Document-level text generation tasks are known to be more difficult than sentence-level text generation tasks as they require the understanding of longer context to generate high-quality texts. | **[Investigating Length Issues in Document-level Machine Translation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17592v2)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2412.17592v2) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>Accepted at the MT Summit 2025</p></details> | 2025-04-28 | **做法**：测量输入长度对文档级MT的影响，发现性能随长度下降且句位靠后质量更差。<br>**作者报告**：两种架构实验显示长度增加性能下降、句位影响质量；改长度分布和位置编码仅边际缓解。<br>**判断**：揭示长文档直译局限，支撑切分/智能体方案的动机。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Transformer architectures are increasingly effective at processing and generating very long chunks of texts, opening new perspectives for document-level machine translation (MT). | **[AppSci-Bench: Can LLMs Build AI Agents and Make Them Better?](http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25360v2)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25360v2) | 2026-11-21 | **做法**：构建 AppSci-Bench，评测 LLM 能否像应用科学家一样为企业构建并改进智能体：需求引导、基础设施探索、实验设计。<br>**作者报告**：七个前沿模型表现弱，最好的 Claude Opus 5.0 三次构建平均仅 59.1%；模型常自评饱和但解不出 held-out 任务。<br>**判断**：4 星：直接评 LLM 构建 agent 的能力，含 Slides Generation 域，与方向④和①有交集。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used not only to power AI agents but also to build them. | **[InfoVis: Unified Slide Image Generation and Editing](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16784v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16784v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>Code: https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/InfoVis</p></details> | 2026-10-12 | **做法**：统一9B幻灯片图像生成与编辑模型InfoVis，配13M+1.9M训练数据和VisSlideBench评测。<br>**作者报告**：开源模型中全11项指标领先，总分7.94对Nano-Banana-2的7.99；后训练提升1.6分。<br>**判断**：视觉生成为主，翻译场景复用有限，可跳过：非翻译任务。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Generating and editing presentation slides requires models to combine accurate text rendering, instruction following, visual grounding, aesthetic design, and precise editing within one canvas. | **[Design First, Code Later: Aesthetically Pleasing Template-Free Slides Generation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26451v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.26451v1) | 2026-04-08 | **做法**：DeepSlides分层工作流先设计后编码，不依赖模板，把页面设计与实现解耦；发布SlideDesign数据集并用多智能体RL训练SlideQwens模型。<br>**作者报告**：称在评测指标上超过基线方法，人评偏好更优；摘要未给数字。<br>**判断**：幻灯片生成全流程含数据集与RL模型，与方向相关但非翻译。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Producing presentation slides automatically entails coordinating narrative structure with page-level graphic design under strict spatial constraints. The Latent Interconnect Protocol (LIP) learns connectors mapping specialist representations into LLM internal states. Training only connectors keeps this practical as LLMs grow and evolve.
